If the employer terminates, the statutory notice period applies if it results in a longer period than the contractually agreed notice period."

      "An employee with an indefinite-term contract who is dismissed due to: reduction or termination of work, reorganization, or incompetence/unsuitability for the function not attributable to their fault, is granted an activation scheme. The scheme consists of an activation budget equal to the statutory transition payment (at least 5000 euros for full-time employment) and a supplement to the Unemployment Insurance Act benefit. The scheme is only granted if the employee is entitled to an Unemployment Insurance Act benefit due to dismissal for one of the mentioned reasons."
    ],
    [

      "The supplement duration is three months, increased by one month for each full year of service beyond three years, up to a maximum of 38 months. For employees with 15+ years of service, the duration is increased by two months, with a maximum of 38 months. For employees reaching AOW-entitlement age within five years of dismissal and with 10+ years of service, the supplement is extended until AOW-entitlement age, based on the IOW-benefit after the WW-period ends (no activation budget in this case)."
    ],
    [
      "Height of Unemployment Insurance Act (WW) supplement",
      "The WW-benefit plus supplement equals the last earned salary for the first 6 months, 80% for the next 3 months, 75% for the next 24 months, and 70% for the subsequent 5 months. For extended benefits (Article 13.4 lid 6), it is 70% of the salary. The salary used for calculation is indexed with CLA wage development."
    ],
    [
      "Forfeiture of WW supplement",
      "The WW supplement ceases upon death, reaching AOW age, claiming disability pension from PFZW, non-compliance with employee obligations (Article 13.6), or cessation of statutory benefits due to employee's inaction. It can also be declared forfeited by the employer if the former employee does not cooperate with medical examination for disability pension/benefit or has permanently settled abroad."
    ],

This definition does not apply to those falling under the C-deel of this CAO."], ["Definition of Region: The geographical area where the employee works, limited to an area of 30 kilometers from the home address at the start of the employment contract or after relocation."], ["Employer obligations (Article 2): The employer must comply with all agreements in this CAO, provide the employee with a copy of the CAO, and request the employee to state upon employment if they have another employment."], ["Employee obligations (Article 3): The employee must comply with all obligations in this CAO, use and properly maintain all tools provided by the employer, return all tools in good condition upon termination of employment, report other employment to their employer, and may not operate their own cleaning business as a self-employed person."], ["Subcontractors (Article 4): Subcontractors must comply with all provisions in the CAO, unless another CAO applies. The contractor controls compliance and the subcontractor must provide requested data."], ["Temporary Employment Agencies (Article 5): The employer must ensure that this CAO is also complied with for temporary workers employed by them. If the temporary employment agency has a NEN4400-1 certificate and the employer keeps a copy, the employer complies. The use of temporary workers may not exceed 7.5% of the total working hours of the company per quarter, excluding detachment via a payroll company, calamity work, and holiday work."], ["Cross-border Work (Article 6): In accordance with the Cross-border Labor Conditions Act, this CAO applies to posted employees performing work in the Netherlands whose employment contract is governed by a law other than Dutch law."], ["Employment Contract (Article 7): Upon employment, the employer is obliged to enter into a written employment contract based on this CAO. It must include: date of employment, duration (fixed or indefinite), initial working hours, weekly hours, job title and corresponding wage group (according to Bijlage II), and the region where the employee is placed."], ["Changes during Employment Contract (Article 8): The employee will accept, within reasonable limits, a change in working hours and/or tasks and/or the object where work is performed and/or the manner in which tasks are performed. The employer will take into account the justified interests of the employee. If an employee performs a different function or if different allowances apply due to changed circumstances, these new allowances apply from the moment of change."], ["Unworkable Weather (Article 33): In deviation from Article 7:628 BW, the employer can assign other than usual tasks, provided they are in line with the activities defined in Article 1 lid 3. In that case, the wage is paid. If this is not possible, the employer will apply for a benefit according to the statutory provision on behalf of the employee. This benefit is supplemented by the employer to 100% of the wage for 27 days, and after 27 days to 90% of the wage."], ["Union Facilities (Article 40): The employer will contact the unions for an intended dismissal (other than for urgent reasons) or intended transfer to another object if one or more of the involved employees request it. The employer will allow paid absence for union officials if the unions request it at least 2 weeks in advance, except in cases of serious business circumstances which will be communicated to the unions. The costs of this absence can be declared by the employer to the RAS. Unions are allowed to train a maximum of 10 officials, distributed across different companies, for 1 month with retention of pay during the CAO term. The absence costs resulting from participation in this training can be declared by the employer to the RAS. Unions have the possibility to consult with employees in the meeting room of the object, observing the following rules: union work takes place outside working hours and does not hinder the progress of work; the intention to visit an object is reported to the employer at least 2 days in advance; no interference, observation, or registration of employees visiting the union meetings will take place."], ["Union Contributions (Article 41): The employee can submit a request to the employer for a reduction of the wage in December of 2010 and 2011 equal to the costs paid by them in the respective calendar year for membership of an employee organization. The employer will grant this request in exchange for an expense reimbursement equal to the paid membership costs. For the Regulation Supplement to the Employment Contract regarding Reimbursement of Membership Costs of an Employee Organization, refer to Bijlage IX."], ["Binding Advice Procedure RAS (Article 42): For the Regulation Disputes Committee RAS, refer to Bijlage VII."], ["Raad voor Arbeidsverhoudingen Schoonmaak- en Glazenwassersbranche (RAS) (Article 45): There is a Stichting Raad voor Arbeidsverhoudingen Schoonmaak- en Glazenwassersbranche (RAS) located at Magistratenlaan 182, Postbus 2216, 5202 CE ’s-Hertogenbosch (e-mail: info@ras.nl). The Statutes and Regulations of the Controle Orgaan are part of the CAO."], ["Dispensation (Article 47): The RAS is authorized, if special circumstances in a company or group of companies give cause, to allow deviations from the provisions of this agreement. The Works Councils Act remains fully applicable."], ["Financing Branch Organs (Article 48): The employer owes an annual contribution to the RAS, the Controle Orgaan, and the O&O-fonds of a certain percentage of the wage sum. This percentage is determined by the RAS based on an established budget. The contribution percentage is set at 0.75% (excluding VAT) of the wage sum in the current year."], ["Commencement and Duration (Article 49): This agreement becomes valid on January 1, 2010. The agreement ends on December 31, 2011, with the exception of Articles 1, 37, 45, 46, and 48, including associated statutes and regulations, which end on December 31, 2013. The employer indicates in writing as soon as possible, but no later than one month before the desired start date, whether the request is granted. The employer deducts 100% employee premium from the salary and pays 100% employee and employer premium to the Bedrijfspensioenfonds. The employee surrenders the extra vacation days due to age and length of service to qualify for this scheme."
    ],

The right to 1 roster-free Saturday per month does not apply to employees working in home furnishing retail."

    [
      "Termination or continuation of CAO",
      "This CAO can be terminated against the end date by one (or more) CAO parties in writing. The termination must be made at least three months before the expiry of this CAO. If the termination is not made in writing or not in time, the CAO will continue for one year at a time."
    ],
    [
      "Compliance and Sociale Commissie",
      "In case of interpretation differences regarding correct compliance with or in case of an application for dispensation from (an aspect/article in) the CAO, an employee and/or an employer, or their authorized representative(s), can contact the Sociale Commissie."
    ],
    [
      "Composition of Sociale Commissie",
      "The Sociale Commissie has 5 (and 5 substitute) members. Two members (and 2 substitutes) are chosen by the employee organization(s). The employer organizations also choose 2 members (and 2 substitutes). To complete the committee, employee and employer organizations choose a fifth independent member. This last member is also chairman of the committee. INretail appoints a secretary who serves the Sociale Commissie, but is not a member of the committee. The secretary's office is located at Arnhemse Bovenweg 100, 3708 AG Zeist."
    ],
    [
      "Tasks of Sociale Commissie",
      "The Sociale Commissie has the following tasks: Granting dispensation from (an aspect/article in) this CAO upon written request from employer or employee; Providing binding advice in case of an interpretation difference regarding the explanation and/or application of this CAO, when employer and employee declare in advance to accept that advice; Providing requested and unrequested advice in case of an ambiguity regarding the application of an article."
    ],
    [
      "Working method of Sociale Commissie for interpretation differences",
      "After written submission of a unilateral request by an employee or an employer, the applicant and the counterparty are asked for written consent to receive a binding advice. After both parties' consent, the counterparty is asked to provide its substantive view on the request. The applicant and the counterparty may, if desirable for the committee members' understanding, be asked to provide additional information on the request or the view based on both parties' positions. The Sociale Commissie will reach a decision through internal consultation and possibly a hearing. The applicant and the counterparty will both be informed of the binding advice in a uniform written manner. If the employer and/or employee declare in advance not to agree to binding advice, the Sociale Commissie will provide written advice to the applicant and send a copy to the counterparty. During the procedure, the parties, being employer and employee, are bound by a response time of a maximum of 4 weeks."
    ],
    [
      "Dispensation by Sociale Commissie",
      "The Sociale Commissie can also be asked by the employer and/or employee to grant dispensation from one or more articles/provisions of the CAO. The Sociale Commissie will in any case test the following points in a dispensation request: The reasons why dispensation is requested must be sufficiently substantiated; The terms of employment for the employees must be sufficiently guaranteed; The terms of employment scheme(s) proposed by the employer may not conflict with legal provisions. The Sociale Commissie will, within 2 weeks of receipt of a sufficiently described, motivated and documented request, or within 2 weeks of receipt of the requested additional information, inform the applicant that the request will be processed. The Sociale Commissie will make a decision as soon as possible, but no later than within 8 weeks after the request has been processed. This period of 8 weeks can be extended once by 8 weeks. The decision will be communicated to the applicant in writing and with reasons."
    ],
    [
      "Senior days",
      "If measures have been developed in the field of sustainable employability that have a longer-term effect, parties will discuss senior days."

    [
      "Working hours research",
      "CAO parties will, during the term of the CAO, conduct research into the wishes and needs of employers and employees in the field of working hours, flexibility and employee control over their working hours. An external research agency, which will be jointly selected by the CAO parties, will be involved in this research. In addition to this research, a self-scheduling pilot will be set up at at least two large companies (one from Fashion and one from Sport), which will be monitored and guided by the CAO parties. The results of this pilot will be evaluated during the discussions in the context of the next CAO."
    ],

      "CAO modernization",
      "CAO parties will, during the term of the CAO, investigate how it can be modernized, so that employers and employees feel more connected to it. Among other things, it will be investigated how more choice options can be created for employees. The possibility of forming an individual training budget will also be investigated. Furthermore, during the term of the CAO, a joint committee will conduct research into the entire system of allowances. The research aims at least to modernize the regulations surrounding public holidays, namely the allowances and the public holiday scheme itself, taking into account the employee's work experience, the possibility of self-scheduling and the changed store opening hours. Social partners will select an external agency for guidance."

The associated digital platform has now been developed and will be further expanded.       "Wonen - Safe transport - Passenger insurance",
      "For vehicles that personnel use for work, the employer must take out passenger insurance for all occupants. This only applies to travel other than normal commuting, but for both own vehicles and those of the employees. That insurance provides at least the following coverages per occupant: upon death €13,613; upon permanent disability €22,689. The costs of this insurance are for the account of the employer. The employee is not obliged to use a vehicle made available to him for which no passenger insurance or a WA-insurance has been taken out."
    ],
    [
      "Wonen - Safe transport - Travel time and costs",
      "For external employees, 30 minutes (one way) is considered own time when traveling directly from their own home to the place of work. External employees are all employees, except for example sales, store, warehouse, cleaning and administrative personnel. For non-external employees, when working at another location, a travel time (one way) of at least 1 hour from home to work or branch is normal. If an employee has to work for work in a municipality other than the usual one, any additional travel time between home and work will be reimbursed at the normal hourly wage. This is not considered overtime. This regulation does not apply to drivers and co-drivers, because traveling is their work. In all cases, the employer reimburses any travel and accommodation costs incurred. This is done afterwards. If possible, the employee provides proof."
    ],
    [
      "Wonen - Temporary other work",
      "An employer can oblige an employee to do other work within the company (within the same branch or another branch) if there is a valid reason for it. The other work is then always temporary and for as long as the reason lasts. Temporary lasts a maximum of 3 months, after which extension is possible in consultation between employer and employee. Temporary other work never results in a decrease in wage. If it concerns a higher classified function for longer than one month, the employee will be paid the scale wage belonging to that function for the duration of the replacement, insofar as this is higher than his normal wage. An employee can only be obliged to work in another company of his employer if that company is commercially related to the company in which the employee works."
    ],

All other tools are also provided on loan by the employer. If the employer does not provide this, the employee receives a reimbursement of €2.60 per week for the purchase and maintenance of these other tools. This amount is indexed annually, as of January 1, with the inflation figure (consumer prices) of two years prior. The next adjustment will take place on January 1, 2018, based on the figures for 2016."

    [
      "Job classification system",
      "This CAO uses a job evaluation system (FUWAM). There are 9 function levels, A to I, and corresponding wage scales. Each function level contains full-fledged functions; each scale can therefore be an end scale. Employees are classified at the correct function level using the Handbook for Job Classification for Fashion, Sport & Lifestyle or the tools made available online by the branch organization, such as the website www.functiewijzer.nu. Appendix 1c contains a summary of the level sheets of that Handbook. The Handbook is available digitally. For employees who do not agree with the classification, there is an objection and appeal procedure (see appendix 1d)."
    ],
    [
      "Own wage structure",
      "Employers who wish to apply their own wage structure can submit a request for dispensation to the Sociale Commissie."
    ],
    [
      "Guaranteed wage determination",
      "The wage paid to an employee is linked to a function group in which the employee's function is classified. The employee receives a wage that is at least equal to the amount determined for his age, or for his function years, according to the wage scales (see also appendix 1b of this CAO). The amounts included therein are gross and are stated both on the basis of an hour worked and per month with an average working time of 38 hours per week, and for Wonen 37 hours per week."
    ],
    [
      "Hourly wage calculation",
      "For the calculation of the hourly wage, the monthly wage is divided by 164.67 and the weekly wage by 38. For Wonen, for the calculation of the hourly wage, the monthly wage is divided by 160.33 and the weekly wage by 37."
    ],
    [
      "Wage adjustment - July 1, 2016",
      "As of July 1, 2016, the wages of employees who have not yet reached the new maximum of their scale will be increased by 0.83%. (See wage structure system, appendix 1a.) The scale wages will be increased by this percentage as of this date."
    ],
    [
      "Wage adjustment - Jan 1, 2017, July 1, 2017, Jan 1, 2018",
      "During the term of the CAO, on January 1, 2017, July 1, 2017 and January 1, 2018, the scale wages and the wages of employees who have not yet reached the new maximum of their scale will be increased by the percentage by which the Statutory Minimum Wage is increased. If the percentage determined by the government on one of the aforementioned dates is lower than 0.4%, the aforementioned wages and wage scales will be increased by 0.4%."

      "Performance-related pay",
      "The employer determines whether performance-related pay is applied. If a works council is present, the employer must consult with it. If it is decided to pay performance-related, the appraisal system applies to all employees aged 22 and older. The system must at least meet established criteria (see appendix 1d). Examples of simple to more complex appraisal systems are offered for the branch. Performance-related pay is possible after a one-year appraisal cycle has taken place."
    ],
    [
      "Wage by age",
      "Wage increase due to age takes effect on the first day of the payment period in which the employee has his birthday. This applies to employees who start employment before their 22nd birthday. This also remains the date on which an experience year is granted after their 22nd birthday."
    ],
    [
      "Experience year",
      "For employees who are 22 years or older when starting employment: Wage increase due to reaching an experience year takes effect on the first day of the payment period in which the employee has one year of experience in the function. If the company applies the system of performance-related pay, the experience year is replaced by the appraisal increase. A company can choose to grant the appraisal increase at a fixed time of the year - this fixed time then applies to all employees - which means that the increase date does not have to coincide with the employee reaching the experience year. In that case, every employee with at least 6 months of experience in a function must be eligible for the appraisal increase."
    ],
    [
      "Experience wage for 21-year-olds",
      "Employees who, at the moment they reach the age of 21, have at least two years of experience in their function at the company, receive the scale wage of a 22-year-old. The provision regarding experience wage for 21-year-olds does not apply to employees working in home furnishing retail."
    ],
    [
      "Placement in another function",
      "If the employee is placed in another function, he will be classified in the function group belonging to that function as of the date of appointment in that function. From that day on, he receives the corresponding wage, unless a functional probation period has been agreed. When placed in a higher function group, the employee is entitled to at least the same wage."
    ],
    [
      "Wage for public holiday",
      "The employee is entitled to continued payment of wages on a public holiday (for definition see art. 2 of this CAO) if he normally performs work on that day. In the case of an irregular work pattern, this can be determined if the employee has performed work on the day in question in at least 8 of the past 13 weeks. In determining this, all realized hours (see article 2) count as worked days. If the criterion of 8 out of 13 is met, the wage will be paid on the basis of the average number of hours worked on those days. If the employee performs work on the public holiday, the allowance scheme of article 7.4 also applies."

      "Tuincentra - Diploma allowance",
      "If an employee follows one of the courses/trainings mentioned below and obtains the corresponding diploma/certificate, he/she receives a monthly allowance on top of his/her applicable guaranteed wage. This also applies to employees who have followed one of these courses/trainings, or an equivalent course/training, in the past. a. Gezond lopen adviseur (Course Advisor for Custom-fit Shoes or an equivalent training) ... €33.70 (*03) b. Gezond Lopen Specialist (Nationally recognized diploma Shoe Consultant or an equivalent training) ... €46.70 (*03). (*03 = If any refresher training for this course/training is offered, the employee is obliged to follow it to retain the allowance.) The employee in part-time employment receives the rights mentioned under point 1 proportionally. In case of overlap of entitlements under point 1, the employee is only entitled to the allowance that grants the employee the most far-reaching rights. If the full-time wage of the employee at the moment he obtains the diploma is higher than the sum of his applicable guaranteed wage and the gross allowance mentioned in point 1, he is not entitled to this allowance. The employee receives this allowance in addition to any other allowances from article 7 of this CAO. This allowance applies regardless of the number of hours he works."

    [
      "Tuincentra - Job classification and wage table",
      "Before December 1, 2016, employers classified the functions of their employees in FUWAM, the job evaluation system in CAO Fashion, Sport & Lifestyle. The website www.functiewijzer.nu can be used as a tool for classifying the functions. Functions in the catering, animal and service desk departments, which cannot yet be classified in FUWAM, will be described before October 1, 2016. On December 31, 2016, employees with their wage at that time will be classified in the wage table Tuincentra Nieuw. The wage scale is equal to the function level determined by classification. However, the classification in the wage table Tuincentra Nieuw only has consequences for the employee's wage when the employee reaches an experience year. The moment the employee reaches an experience year in 2017, the following happens: If the employee then turns 23 or is older than 23, he/she fictitiously moves to the next step in the wage table Tuincentra bestaande medewerkers and then to the next higher step in the wage table Tuincentra Nieuw. (If there is no next step or no next higher step (i.e. the employee is or becomes above scale), he/she retains the wage or that of the next step respectively.) If the employee is not yet 23, he/she moves to the step in the wage table Tuincentra Nieuw corresponding to his/her age. If it appears that the wage development perspective after classification in the wage table of the Tuincentra Nieuw module is worse for the employee than if the wage table Tuincentra bestaande medewerkers would have remained applicable, he/she is still entitled to a guarantee of the old perspective for five years, as follows: During the five years after the classification in the wage table of the Tuincentra Nieuw module has been carried out as described above, the employee's table wage (as described above) is annually compared with the wage he/she would have had if the wage table Tuincentra bestaande medewerkers had remained applicable, with the understanding that a wage increase of 0.4% as of January 1, 2017 and all wage increases of the CAO Fashion, Sport & Lifestyle as of July 1, 2017 apply to it. If this wage (that he/she would have had in the indexed wage table Tuincentra bestaande medewerkers) is higher, then that higher wage becomes his/her new wage. After the compensation period of 5 years (December 31, 2021), the employee will not experience a decrease in wage. For youth wages up to 22 years in the wage table Tuincentra Nieuw, these will be 5% higher than the corresponding youth wages in the wage table Mode in CAO Fashion, Sport & Lifestyle. The moment the government adjusts legislation or regulations in the future regarding the equalization of youth wages for 22-year-olds and younger to the Statutory Minimum Wage for 23-year-olds, the 5% difference for 22-year-olds will lapse."

If the substitute has already reached the maximum of the periodic increases or if his salary is higher than his function classification prescribes, he receives an amount equal to twice the difference between function year 23/0 and 23/1 within his current salary scale for the duration of the substitution. An employee does not receive extra substitution wage for: replacement of a colleague during vacation; replacement that lasts less than 1 month."

      "Implementation on next higher step on a date to be determined that may differ from the implementation date of the new wage structure. CAO increases apply to the wage table.       "Implementation agreements and wage structure system - Performance-related pay (wage above highest step of extra wage structure)",
      "No standard CAO increases apply. Individual agreements between employer and employee."
    ],
    [
      "Implementation agreements and wage structure system - Non-performance-related pay (wage within basic wage structure)",
      "Implementation on next higher step on a date to be determined that may differ from the implementation date of the new wage structure. CAO increases apply to the wage table. Employees are entitled to a step increase after each subsequent experience year, regardless of the degree of functioning, up to the maximum of the basic step of the wage scale."

      "Implementation agreements and wage structure system - Non-performance-related pay (wage above highest step of extra wage structure)",

      "Implementation agreements and wage structure system - Exclusions for performance-related pay",
      "Employees younger than 23 years are not performance-related paid, but only receive their age-related increase."

    [
      "Criteria for performance-related pay - Job content",
      "There is clarity and agreement between manager and employee about the employee's function (tasks and responsibilities). This is recorded in writing in a job description."
    ],
    [
      "Criteria for performance-related pay - Performance discussion frequency",
      "At least once a year (preferably twice a year) a discussion with the employee about performance takes place. If once a year: The direct manager conducts at least 1 discussion per year with the employee about performance. During this discussion, at least the following elements are covered: Based on the job description, the manager makes agreements with the employee about the expectations for the coming year (looking ahead). The current performance in relation to the agreements made is discussed. If necessary, agreements are adjusted or the manager decides to provide more guidance to achieve the results (present). The manager gives an opinion on the employee's performance, partly based on the agreements made (past). The employee's performance in the past period is evaluated and based on this, the consequences for the remuneration of the coming year are determined. The discussion will in most cases start with giving an opinion on the employee's performance over a preceding period. Following this opinion, the current performance can be discussed and new agreements can be made for the next (appraisal) period. If twice a year: The direct manager conducts at least 1 performance review per year AND at least 1 appraisal interview per year with the employee. During the performance review, at least the following elements are covered: Based on the job description, the manager and employee make agreements about the expectations for the coming year (looking ahead). The current performance and the agreements made are discussed. If necessary, agreements are adjusted or the manager decides to provide more guidance to achieve the results (present). In the performance review, employee and employer are equal discussion partners. Performance reviews can be held more often during the year as needed. During the appraisal interview, the manager gives an opinion on the employee's performance based on the agreements made (past). The employee's performance in the past period is evaluated and based on this, the consequences for the remuneration of the coming year are determined. In the appraisal interview, the manager and employee are unequal discussion partners, because the manager expresses an opinion on the employee's performance. The employee's input is limited."
    ],
    [
      "Criteria for performance-related pay - Written procedure",
      "The appraisal procedure is in writing and known to employees. The procedure surrounding the discussion, the purpose and meaning of the discussion and the consequences it can have for the employee, must be in writing and known to the employee and, in case of a works council, also have the approval of this body. It must be clear in advance which criteria apply to the appraisal. The possible appraisal outcomes (e.g., unsatisfactory, moderate, normal/good, very good and excellent) are clearly described and put in writing and, with regard to the salary consequences, clear to everyone. For the appraisal outcomes, it must at least be clearly established when a 'satisfactory' and when an 'unsatisfactory' appraisal is given. This is in connection with possible salary consequences as described in appendix 1c of the CAO. The judgment on the performance of employees and other agreements made during appraisal interviews are recorded in writing. The appraisal system is regularly evaluated by management (and representative body)."

  "pension_information": [
    [
      "Pension scheme details",
      "The employer is obliged, on the basis of a mandatory declaration from the Minister of Social Affairs and Employment, to be affiliated with the Bedrijfstakpensioenfonds Detailhandel (Industry Pension Fund Retail), unless he has been dispensed or exempted. Conditions may be attached to the exemption. The Stichting Bedrijfstakpensioenfonds Detailhandel (Foundation Industry Pension Fund Retail) is responsible for the implementation of the pension scheme. The board of the foundation has entrusted the management and administration of the pension scheme to TKP in Groningen. This body also collects the premiums for the pension fund. Further information about the pension scheme can be obtained from TKP, Postbus 1233, 9701 BE Groningen, telephone 050-5225098. The Bedrijfstakpensioenfonds Detailhandel also has an internet site: www.pensioendetailhandel.nl. The above article on pension does not apply to home furnishing retail (woondetailhandel)."
    ],

  "leave_information": [
    [
      "Vacation year",
      "The vacation year coincides with the calendar year, unless other agreements have been made with the works council. Vacation rights accrue over this period."
    ],
    [
      "Vacation hours with retention of wage",
      "The employee is entitled to vacation with retention of wage for 182.4 hours per year (based on a working week of an average of 38 hours or a working day of an average of 7.6 hours). For Wonen, this is 185 hours (based on a working week of an average of 37 hours or a working day of an average of 7.4 hours). The vacation rights are deemed to accrue proportionally over the year. The employee who is employed by the employer for only part of the vacation year is entitled to a proportional part of the vacation mentioned in this article."
    ],
    [
      "Accrual of vacation hours",
      "For the accrual of vacation hours, the number of agreed contract hours (or basic hours) is generally used as a starting point. If the employee works more than his basic hours on the basis of a vacation year, vacation hours also accrue on the excess, unless the extra hours are taken as time off in accordance with article 4."
    ],
    [
      "Determination of vacation hours to be taken",
      "When determining the number of hours to be taken for (a) vacation (day), the number of hours the employee would work according to his schedule is generally used as a starting point. If no schedule has been determined, the number of vacation hours to be taken in the relevant week is equal to the number of hours per week that the employee has worked on average in the last 13 weeks preceding the vacation. If that reference period is not representative, the last 52 weeks preceding the day of the vacation will be used."
    ],
    [
      "Consecutive vacation",
      "The employee is entitled to two consecutive weeks of vacation per calendar year."
    ],
    [
      "Vacation during sickness",
      "If the employee has received permission to go on vacation during the sickness period (see also the absence regulations in appendix 2 of this CAO), these days will be deducted from the vacation balance."
    ],
    [
      "Expiration of statutory vacation days",
      "Statutory vacation days expire 6 months after the calendar year in which they were acquired, but the employer may, in consultation with the works council, deviate from this expiration period and then apply the old expiration period of 5 years. The agreed regulation then applies to all employees."
    ],
    [
      "Collective vacation days",
      "The employer may, in consultation with the works council, designate a maximum of 2 days per year as mandatory collective vacation days for the company. Deviations per location are possible."
    ],
    [
      "Extra vacation hours due to age",
      "The employee aged: 50 to 55 years is entitled to extra vacation hours equal to 1/5 of the number of basic hours per week; 55 to 60 years is entitled to extra vacation hours equal to 2/5 of the number of basic hours per week; 60 years or older is entitled to extra vacation hours equal to 3/5 of the number of basic hours per week, unless there is an adjustment of working hours in accordance with article 10. In that case, these extra vacation hours lapse. The employee accrues the extra vacation hours per vacation year."
    ],
    [
      "Extra vacation hours due to length of service",
      "The employee with an employment of: 25 to 40 years is entitled to extra vacation hours equal to 2/5 of the number of basic hours per week; 40 years or more is entitled to extra vacation hours equal to 4/5 of the number of basic hours per week. The employee accrues the extra vacation hours per vacation year."
    ],
    [
      "Overlap of extra vacation hours",
      "If the extra vacation hours scheme due to age overlaps with the extra vacation hours scheme due to the duration of employment, only the scheme that is most favorable to the employee applies."
    ],
    [
      "Retention of wage for extra vacation hours",
      "The employee retains the right to his normal wage for the extra vacation hours."
    ],
    [
      "Holiday allowance",
      "The holiday allowance year runs from June 1 to May 31. The employer may agree on a different holiday allowance year in consultation with the works council. The employee who has been employed for the entire holiday allowance year receives a holiday allowance of 8% of the actually earned wage in the holiday allowance year, no later than in June."
    ],
    [
      "Special leave - General",
      "Special leave enables an employee to attend special events, particularly in the private sphere. For a part-timer, this means the right to leave for the hours he would normally work on the day(s) concerned."
    ],
    [
      "Special leave - Marriage",
      "In addition to the statutory leave regulations from the Wet Arbeid & Zorg, the employee is entitled to special leave with retention of wage in the following cases: for his marriage: on the day of the marriage and the day before or after; for attending the marriage of one of his children (including step- and foster children), brothers and sisters as well as one of the parents (in-law): the day of the marriage; for his 25th, 40th, 50th and 60th wedding anniversary and that of his parents and parents-in-law: the day of the anniversary or the day on which the celebration takes place."
    ],
    [
      "Special leave - Death",
      "In addition to the statutory leave regulations from the Wet Arbeid & Zorg, the employee is entitled to special leave with retention of wage in the following cases: for the death of the spouse or of own resident children: from the day of death up to and including the day of the funeral; for the death of one of the parents, parents-in-law, own non-resident children, foster or step-children: the day on which the funeral takes place and the day of death or a day between the death and the funeral; for attending the funeral of grandparents, partner's grandparents, grandchildren, brothers, sisters, brothers-in-law and sisters-in-law: the day of the funeral."
    ],
    [
      "Special leave - Service anniversary",
      "In addition to the statutory leave regulations from the Wet Arbeid & Zorg, the employee is entitled to special leave with retention of wage in the following cases: for the employee's 25th, 40th and 50th service anniversary: 1 day."
    ],
    [
      "Special leave - Relocation",
      "In addition to the statutory leave regulations from the Wet Arbeid & Zorg, the employee is entitled to special leave with retention of wage in the following cases: for relocation: the day of relocation."
    ],
    [
      "Special leave - Job application",
      "In addition to the statutory leave regulations from the Wet Arbeid & Zorg, the employee is entitled to special leave with retention of wage in the following cases: if the employment has been terminated by the employer: for a reasonable period for applying for a new job and verbally explaining the application."
    ],
    [
      "Special leave - Medical reason",
      "In addition to the statutory leave regulations from the Wet Arbeid & Zorg, the employee is entitled to special leave with retention of wage in the following cases: The starting point is that the employee makes appointments in his own time. If that is not possible, there is a right to special leave for a mutually agreed reasonable period: for undergoing a mandatory medical examination; and for necessary visits by the employee to the doctor, dentist or specialist."
    ],
    [
      "Special leave - Sustainable cohabitation",
      "The employee who has already entered into a legally registered partnership or a sustainable cohabitation is equated with the married employee."
    ],
    [
      "Absence regulations - General",
      "In case of illness or accident due to which the employee cannot perform work, the absence regulations established in the company must be observed. If such regulations are missing, the standard absence regulations Fashion, Sport & Lifestyle (appendix 2 of this CAO) apply. Changes to these regulations can be agreed upon with the works council."
    ],
    [
      "Absence regulations - Continued wage payment",
      "The employee is entitled to continued wage payment of 100% of the gross wage during the first 26 weeks of incapacity for work. In the subsequent period of 26 weeks (week 27 to 52), 90% of the gross wage, but at least the statutory minimum wage, will be paid. In the period of 26 weeks thereafter (week 53 to 78), 80% of the gross wage will be paid. In the period of 26 weeks thereafter (week 79 to 104), the statutory wage payment obligation of 70% of the gross wage applies."
    ],
    [
      "Absence regulations - Waiting days",
      "Per sickness case, 1 waiting day can be deducted. Periods of sickness are aggregated if they follow each other with an interruption of less than four weeks. The employer does not have to pay wages for a waiting day. The employer is allowed to offset the waiting day with vacation hours that have been granted to the employee above the statutory minimum, with the wage or with compensation days/hours, unless otherwise agreed with the employee representation body. A maximum of four waiting days per year can be offset with vacation hours or deducted from the wage. If the company doctor/occupational health physician (being a doctor from the company's occupational health service) has diagnosed a chronic illness in the employee, the employer will be extremely reluctant to deduct waiting days."
    ],
    [
      "Absence regulations - Determination of wage payment during sickness and waiting day",
      "When determining the number of paid sick hours per day and the number of hours for the waiting day, the number of hours the employee would have worked according to his schedule is used as a starting point. If the employee does not work in a store and the working hours cannot be determined in advance, caused by external circumstances (e.g., for drivers), the number of contract hours per week can be used instead of the scheduled hours. If no schedule has been determined, the number of hours the employee has worked on average per day in the last 13 weeks preceding the incapacity for work will be used as a starting point. If that reference period is not representative in the employer's opinion, the last 52 weeks preceding the day of sickness will be used."
    ],
    [
      "Absence regulations - Consecutive sickness cases",
      "Sickness cases that follow each other within a period of 4 weeks are considered one sickness case, regardless of the cause of the sickness."
    ],
    [
      "Absence regulations - Settlement of social insurance benefits",
      "Any benefits under social insurance laws that will be paid through the employer will be deducted from the wage payments."
    ],
    [
      "Absence regulations - End of wage payment",
      "The wage payments mentioned in this article end at the end of the employment contract of the employee concerned."
    ],
    [
      "Absence regulations - Transfer of rights",
      "If the employer is obliged to continue paying wages during incapacity for work, the employee must transfer his rights against third parties (including those of the UWV) due to wage loss due to incapacity for work to the employer."
    ],
    [
      "Absence regulations - Expense reimbursement and incapacity",
      "If the incapacity for work lasts longer than one month consecutively, the employer does not have to pay expense reimbursements directly related to the work after that month."
    ],
    [
      "Tuincentra - Vacation days",
      "A full-timer gets 25 vacation days each year."
    ],
    [
      "Tuincentra - ATV (Working time reduction)",
      "A full-timer is entitled to 13 roster-free days if his normal working hours are 40 hours per week. In mutual consultation, employer and employee determine how these hours are taken (in the form of 13 full days, 26 half days or 104 hours)."
    ],
    [
      "Wonen - Vacation days",
      "A full-timer gets 25 vacation days per vacation year. That is 185 hours, based on a working week of 37 hours, or an average working day of 7.4 hours. The consecutive vacation usually consists of 15 working days. Employer and employees can agree to shorten this period, but never to less than 10 working days. If an employee has accrued too few hours to take the consecutive vacation, the missing time counts as unpaid leave. The employer can, in consultation with his employees or the employee representation body, set a maximum of three weeks of collective leave. Employers in floor laying companies may oblige their employees to take time off between Christmas and New Year if no work can be done due to the closure of construction companies."
    ],
    [
      "Wonen - Extra vacation days for BBL students",
      "A BBL student who is 18 or 19 years old on May 1 and regularly attends vocational education during working hours, receives 37 extra vacation hours per vacation year, if the vocational education is directly related to his function."
    ],
    [
      "Wonen - Extra vacation days by age",
      "From the year in which a full-time employee turns 50, he is entitled to 26 vacation days per year. In the year in which the full-time employee turns 55, he is entitled to 27 vacation days per year. In the year in which the full-time employee turns 56, he is entitled to 29 vacation days. Part-timers receive the extra free hours pro rata to their employment."
    ],
    [
      "Wonen - Special leave - General",
      "In a number of special circumstances, wages are continued to be paid. The special leave is only granted on the day the event takes place, unless stated otherwise. Married persons, persons living together permanently and registered partners are treated equally."
    ],
    [
      "Wonen - Special leave - Marriage",
      "Special leave with wage retention for: for the employee's engagement: 1 day; if the employee has been married for 25 or 40 years: 1 day; if the employee's parents, parents-in-law, foster or step-parents have been married for 25, 40, 50 or 60 years: 1 day; for attending the marriage of a parent, parent-in-law, child, stepchild, foster child, brother, sister, brother-in-law or sister-in-law of the employee: 1 day; for the employee's marriage: 2 days."
    ],
    [
      "Wonen - Special leave - Death",
      "Special leave with wage retention for: for attending the funeral of a brother, sister, brother-in-law, sister-in-law, grandparent or grandchild: 1 day; for the death of a non-resident parent, step-parent, parent-in-law, child, stepchild, foster child: one day and for attending the funeral another day: 2 days; for the death of the partner, a child, a parent or parent-in-law with whom the employee lives: from the moment of death up to and including the day of the funeral."
    ],
    [
      "Wonen - Special leave - Special cases",
      "Special leave with wage retention for: if the employee has been employed by the same employer for 25 or 40 years: 1 day; if the employee moves house: once a year 1 day; if the government does not provide reimbursement for going to the polling station or fulfilling civic obligations: the necessary time; if the government does not provide reimbursement for fulfilling military obligations: a reasonable time; the employee can attend the retirement course for a maximum of 1 year before the pension date: a maximum of one week; if an employee has to visit a doctor or dentist, this should be done as much as possible outside working hours."
    ],
    [
      "Wonen - Bereavement leave",
      "The employer can, in consultation with the employee, and if necessary in consultation with the occupational health service, grant time off with retention of wage to process the loss of a partner, parent, parent-in-law, foster or step-parent, child, step or foster child, brother, sister, brother-in-law or sister-in-law. The manner in which the leave is used and its duration are discussed in mutual consultation between employer and employee."
    ]

  "termination_information": [
    [
      "Probation period",
      "For a fixed-term employment contract of more than 6 months and for an indefinite-term employment contract, the probation period during the term of this CAO is two months. During this period, the employment contract can be terminated without further ado with immediate effect by the employer or by the employee. No dismissal permit is required and no notice period applies. For a fixed-term employment contract of 6 months or less, no probation period can be agreed upon."
    ],
    [
      "Chain rule for temporary workers",
      "If an employer hires a former temporary worker who immediately prior to this worked as a temporary worker for the employer, the various temporary employment contracts are considered as one employment contract for the combined duration of the temporary employment contracts. The employee who, after having worked as a temporary worker for the employer, immediately enters into employment with the employer, therefore starts the second chain contract."
    ],
    [
      "End of employment contract due to age",
      "The employment contract ends without notice on the day the AOW-entitlement age is reached."
    ],
    [
      "Flexible deployment - Termination during reference period",
      "If, upon termination of employment, it appears that the employee has worked more hours than he has received wages for, these hours will be paid out. These extra hours will be paid out at the normal wage applicable to the employee (including holiday allowance). Remaining fewer hours at the end of the employment contract will be settled in the final settlement, unless these were caused by circumstances within the employer's sphere of influence. Upon termination of the employment contract by the employee, he must cooperate in ensuring that any remaining extra/fewer hours can be scheduled before the end of the employment contract."
    ]
  ],
  "overtime_information": [
    [
      "Overtime definition",
      "Overtime occurs if the employee, after consultation between the employer and the employee, has been instructed to perform work: for more than 10 hours per day; or for more than 40 hours per week, calculated over a period of four consecutive weeks. The pattern of the four-week periods is the same for all employees in the company."
    ],
    [
      "Overtime obligation and allowance",
      "The employer prevents the employee from having to work overtime as much as possible, but can legally demand this from the employee. The employee is then obliged to perform the work. In this case, the employee is entitled to an overtime allowance. See article 7.5."
    ],
    [
      "Incidental overtime exclusion",
      "In the case of plannable and/or foreseeable work, an incidental exceeding of the aforementioned hour limits by fifteen minutes or less is not considered overtime. The overtime limits apply to all employees."
    ],
    [
      "Overtime allowance exclusion",
      "A full-time employee with a gross monthly wage that is higher than twice the statutory gross minimum wage per month (for employees aged 23 and older) is not eligible for overtime allowance. Worked overtime hours only count once for determining whether overtime has occurred."
    ],
    [
      "Allowance for certain hours Mon-Fri",
      "The allowance percentages for work from Monday to Friday are: from 00:00 to 07:00: 50%; from 22:00 to 24:00: 50%."
    ],
    [
      "Allowance for certain hours Sat",
      "The allowance percentages for work on Saturday are: from 00:00 to 07:00: 50%; from 22:00 to 24:00: 50%."
    ],
    [
      "Compensation for Saturday evening work",
      "If employees worked more than 20 times on Saturday evening between July 1, 2015 and June 30, 2016, remain willing to do so and received an allowance, the reduction of the allowance will be compensated in a wage increase or in time off. The employer decides on the method of compensation after consultation with the employee. The compensation is calculated over the number of hours worked on Saturday evening between 18:00 and 22:00 in the aforementioned period for which a 100% allowance was paid. The compensation in time off amounts to this number of hours on an annual basis. This is converted to a monthly basis and added to an employee's monthly leave accrual. The compensation in a monthly wage increase is calculated by dividing the gross amount earned for Saturday evening allowance for hours between 18:00 and 22:00 by 12."
    ],
    [
      "Allowance for certain hours Sun and Public Holidays",
      "Working on Sundays and public holidays is voluntary. The employer can have the employee work on Sunday if the employee agrees (in accordance with the Working Hours Act). If the employer cannot staff public holidays that do not fall on a Sunday with volunteers, he can oblige an employee to work, unless the employee has demonstrable conscientious objections. The allowance for work on Sunday is 50%. (* In the CAO with a term from July 1, 2015 to July 1, 2016, an agreement was made about compensation for the reduction of the Sunday allowance to 50%. This agreement can be found on werkindewinkel.nl.) The allowance for work on public holidays is 100%."
    ],
    [
      "Overtime allowance rates",
      "The allowance percentages for overtime are: for more than 10 hours per day: 25%; for every hour above 40 hours per week, calculated over a period of four consecutive weeks: 50%."
    ],
    [
      "Tuincentra - Sunday allowance",
      "The Sunday allowance will be 50% as of September 1, 2016. The employee who worked on Sunday in the period from April 1, 2015 to April 1, 2016, remains willing to do so and received a 100% allowance for the hours worked on Sunday in the aforementioned period, will receive compensation for the reduction of the Sunday allowance to 50%. The compensation is granted as a wage increase or in time off. The employer decides on the method of compensation after consultation with the employee. The compensation is calculated over the number of hours worked on Sunday in the period from April 1, 2015 to April 1, 2016 for which a 100% allowance was paid. The compensation in time off amounts to half of this number of hours on an annual basis. This is converted to a monthly basis and added to an employee's monthly leave accrual. The compensation in a monthly wage increase is calculated by dividing half of the gross amount earned for Sunday allowance by 12. At the employee's request, the employer and employee can jointly adjust the company-wide agreement on the compensation scheme individually (time instead of money; money instead of time). A valid argument for adjustment is a demonstrable financial deterioration (such as the loss of rent and/or care allowance) of the employee's position at the time of implementation of the compensation scheme. Such an adjustment must have been requested by the employee within 3 months of the effective date of the compensation scheme. The agreement on compensation for the reduction of the Sunday allowance does not apply to on-call employees. In case of long-term illness or pregnancy, the reference period is April 1, 2014 to April 1, 2016."
    ],
    [
      "Tuincentra - Deviation from normal working hours",
      "The normal working hours are the standard number of hours to be worked for a full-timer and amount to 38 hours per week. For four months per year, the standard number of hours to be worked per week can be extended to 45 hours per week. This period is determined individually by the employer per employee, possibly divided into a maximum of four periods of at least 1 month. This period(s) is/are determined and communicated to the employee at least three months in advance. During these period(s), no vacation days or roster-free days can be taken."
    ],

Unless the employee wants to take vacation during this period and the employer approves. Overtime is additionally compensated in time or money. For each hour of overtime worked, the employee receives an allowance on the normal hourly wage. The amount of the allowance is: 33.33% for overtime from Monday to Friday from 7:00 to 24:00 and on Saturday from 7:00 to 18:00, if one works more than 9 hours per day and more than 40 hours per week; 100% for overtime between 24:00 and 7:00 for employees who structurally work nights. Overtime allowances do not apply to managers and to employees who are classified in function group V or VI in the garden center CAO. After classification in FUWAM, it remains applicable that overtime does not apply to managers. It is the employer's responsibility to develop and implement such a policy and to periodically evaluate the policy pursued with the works council or employee representation. It is the employee's responsibility to actively cooperate with this policy. Regular performance reviews will take place, which, with regard to the employee, are intended, among other things, to timely identify problems that the employee experiences with aging in his or her functioning and, in consultation with the person concerned, to take measures aimed at both preventing expected problems and solving existing problems. If a previously mentioned activity leads to negative effects on employment, the employer and the trade unions will have to work out arrangements aimed at limiting the adverse consequences for employees resulting from these activities as much as possible. Seeking alternative employment must be part of these arrangements. In the application of this article, the provisions of the Wet op de Ondernemingsraden and the WMCO, as well as the decision of the SER concerning merger conduct rules, remain fully applicable. If there is a dismissal in an undertaking where the WMCO applies, the employer will not invoke any agreed non-compete clause against the employees concerned."
    ],
    [
      "Labor market and FKB (social fund): Goal: Employers and employee organizations want to pursue an effective labor market policy. Both the management and the financing thereof are carried out by the FKB. CAO parties will further elaborate on the labor market policy during the term of this CAO. The employer is obliged to contribute annually to the Stichting Fonds Kollektieve Belangen voor de Technische Groothandel a percentage of the gross wage sum applicable to the undertaking for the current calendar year, to be determined annually by the Foundation Board. The implementation of the FKB is entrusted to the Stichting Fonds Kollektieve Belangen voor de Technische Groothandel."
    ],
    [
      "New social fund FTG: In 2024, a new social fund named the Stichting Fonds Technische Groothandel (also named “Stichting FTG”) will be established by the parties concluding this Arbeidsvoorwaarden-CAO. The Stichting FTG is the intended successor to the Stichting FKB in due course. Only the CAO parties mentioned in paragraph 1 will be members of the board of the Stichting FTG. In 2024 and 2025, a levy of 0.1% of the employer's wage sum will be imposed for the financing of the Stichting FTG, which will be borne by the employer. The levy from 2026 will be determined by the CAO parties in due course. The new FTG will also register all employees in the Technische Groothandel with the aim of proactively informing employees about initiatives, opportunities and developments within the Technische Groothandel. Registration will take place in compliance with AVG legislation."

      "Union work in the company: Trade unions also individually have the right to designate and have functioning cadre members working in the undertaking as contact person(s) in that undertaking: <25 employees: 1 per trade union with a total maximum of 2; 25-50 employees: 2 per trade union with a total maximum of 4; 51-100 employees: 2 per trade union with a total maximum of 6; >100 employees: 3 per trade union with a total maximum of 8. If a trade union proceeds to designate one or more contact person(s), the management of the undertaking concerned will be informed immediately. Contact persons maintain daily contact between the members working in the undertaking and the trade union they represent. In consultation with the management of the undertaking concerned, contact persons can disseminate information from the trade union concerning the sector, the undertaking and/or the trade union organization in the undertaking. Contact persons will not be harmed in their personal interests as a result of their activities for the trade union. If business and/or economic reasons necessitate measures that also affect contact persons, the employer will consult with the salaried director(s) of the trade union at an early stage. Termination of the employment contract with a contact person at the initiative of the management of the undertaking can only take place via and with the consent of the subdistrict court judge."

      "Union facilities: The granting and use of facilities within the undertaking to trade unions relate to: The provision of notice boards and intranet at designated places for: making announcements of a business and informative nature regarding the own undertaking or the own sector; announcing the names of representatives or contact persons of the trade union; announcing meetings of trade unions; publishing concise reports of these meetings; nomination of members of the works council, the content of the messages and announcements to be published will be brought to the attention of the undertaking's management in advance. For written communication with members, contact persons and/or cadre members of the trade unions can use word processing and copying equipment, if available in the undertaking. The provision of meeting space in the undertaking, for meetings of the association body and in general for maintaining contacts with members of trade unions in the undertaking. This must be requested from the undertaking's management in good time. The use of this meeting space generally takes place outside or immediately after normal working hours. In the context of an individual appointment of a member of a trade union, he or she can meet with a director of his or her trade union in the undertaking, provided the employee notifies the employer of this appointment in advance and the business situation permits it. The appointment preferably takes place outside working hours or immediately after normal working hours. The undertaking's visitor policy remains fully applicable to this appointment. The exercise of the activities in the undertaking of trade unions and the granting and use of facilities for this purpose must not disrupt the proper functioning of the undertaking. In special circumstances, when business interest, in the opinion of the undertaking's management, requires it, the granting of facilities can be suspended. This will only happen after prior consultation between the undertaking's management and the trade union(s) concerned. If a difference of opinion arises about the manner of granting and using facilities in the undertaking and the agreements made, any of the parties concerned can submit this difference of opinion to the Vaste Commissie of the CAO and request its good offices. This article is not legally enforceable by the members of one party if the article has not been declared generally applicable."

      "Income definition: Under income is understood the fixed gross (monthly) salary agreed between employer and employee under the employment contract. Under income for sales representatives is understood the agreed fixed gross monthly salary, increased by any commission."

      "Annual income definition: Under annual income is understood the income referred to in paragraph 1 or paragraph 2, converted to an annual amount, increased by: vacation allowance, diploma allowance, allowance for regularly recurring overtime, shift allowance, other fixed allowances; performance bonus; fixed gratuity."
    ],
    [
      "Wage definition (Art 47 lid 1 CAO): Under wage in the sense of Article 47 paragraph 1 CAO is understood the monetary income from employment, with the exception of: earnings from overtime, vacation allowance, profit distributions, payments on special occasions, payments resulting from entitlements to receive one or more payments after a period of time or under a condition, reimbursements insofar as they can be deemed to cover necessary costs that the employee has to incur in connection with his or her employment, special reimbursements for breadwinners and heads of household, payments pursuant to a premium savings scheme as referred to in Article 31a, first paragraph, of the Wet op de loonbelasting 1964, or, a year-end bonus."
    ],
    [
      "Minimum wage note: For paragraph 4, the provisions of Article 16, second paragraph, Wet minimumloon en minimumvakantiebijslag apply without prejudice: the guarantee that the sum of the wage (including overtime) and vacation allowance must be at least 108% of the minimum wage. This means that an employee at minimum wage level is in any case entitled to a vacation allowance of 8% of his or her wage (including overtime)."
    ],
    [
      "On-call workers: In case of appointment of an employee on an on-call basis, the employer must offer at least 16 hours of work per month. If, when called by the employer, the employee is prevented from coming to work, no salary is due for the hours offered on that occasion. If no call takes place during a calendar month or if employment has taken place for less than 16 hours per calendar month, the employer owes at least 16 hours of salary to the on-call worker."
    ],

      "Part-time work: If a part-time employee works longer than the agreed working hours within the normal daily working hours, the salary for the overtime hours concerned must be increased by any other fixed income elements such as the vacation allowance."
    ],

      "Temporary agency workers: Temporary agency workers are paid according to the function group in which the temporary agency worker should be classified based on his or her work to be performed and young people at the youth salary in the applicable function group. The wage and other allowances; b. The working hours, including overtime, rest periods, night shifts, breaks, the duration of vacation and working on public holidays."
    ],
    [
      "Sales representatives salary: To the employee with an employment relationship as a sales representative, from the first year of employment, calculated over a year, at least an average amount of salary plus any commission will be paid per month in accordance with the salary mentioned in the CAO-schaal (see CAO-Bijlage) at 0 periodieken in scale V. Per year of service, this salary increases by at least 1 periodiek per year to a minimum of 7 periodieken in scale V after 7 years of service. Sales representatives in external service will in any case receive the wage increases from Article 32 paragraph 1. Individually granted wage increases may be offset by the employer against the CAO wage increases."

This enumeration is only indicative, not binding and no rights can be derived from it. The description of the activities is decisive for the classification. The final classification is made, after consultation with the employee, by the employer. Written notification will be given to the employee when the function group changes. Function group descriptions are provided for groups 0 to VI, detailing required experience, knowledge, and examples of job titles and activities."
